- **cowtree**: Create, inspect and clean up copy-on-write git worktrees with the `cowtree` CLI. Use whenever a task needs its own branch or worktree, when several branches/PRs of one repository are worked on in parallel, when asked which worktree a branch lives in, or when worktrees need sweeping after merges. Never run plain `git worktree add` in a repo that uses cowtree. ([paakofiaidoo/cowtree](https://github.com/paakofiaidoo/cowtree/tree/HEAD/skills/cowtree) · ★1)
- **cowtree**: Use when creating a Git worktree and disk usage matters, especially when several worktrees share a large checkout on Linux or macOS. ([raiscan/cowtree](https://github.com/raiscan/cowtree/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **cox**: Cox 编程 - 为您的 AI 编程体验保驾护航。帮助开发团队掌握项目进展、识别开发风险、了解系统健康状态。提供项目进度跟踪、迭代管理（MVP驱动）、任务状态管理、开发假设记录、应用模块监控、测试埋点和异常分析等功能。支持静态网页和交互网页两种方案，适合不同环境和团队规模。网页按迭代分组展示，清晰呈现每个迭代的进度和任务。 ([ewanyuan/cox-coding](https://github.com/ewanyuan/cox-coding/tree/HEAD/cn/cox) · ★45)
- **cox**: Cox Programming - Safeguarding your AI programming experience. Helps development teams grasp project progress, identify development risks, and understand system health status. Provides project progress tracking, iteration management (MVP-driven), task status management, development assumption recording, application module monitoring, test tracing points, and anomaly analysis functions. Supports both static web and interactive web solutions, suitable for different environments and team sizes. Web pages are grouped by iteration, clearly presenting the progress and tasks of each iteration. ([ewanyuan/cox-coding](https://github.com/ewanyuan/cox-coding/tree/HEAD/en/cox) · ★45)
- **cox-proportional-hazards-covariate-adjustment**: Use when you have per-sample features (e.g. Miscell latent features computed from TCGA bulk expression) joined to overall survival time/status plus confounders such as Age, Sex and TNM stage, and need covariate-adjusted prognostic associations per feature. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cox-proportional-hazards-covariate-adjustment))
- **cox-proportional-hazards-model-fitting**: Use when individual-level cohort data with time-to-death follow-up (age at blood sampling plus mortality status) must be modeled to estimate hazard ratios or to extract Cox coefficients as weights for a mortality risk score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cox-proportional-hazards-model-fitting))
- **cox-proportional-hazards-modeling**: Use when you have per-sample mutation status merged with survival/time-to-event group annotations and need to test whether a mutation is an independent prognostic factor after adjusting for other covariates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cox-proportional-hazards-modeling))
- **cox-proportional-hazards-mortality-modeling**: Use when relating log-transformed, SD-scaled biomarker measurements (e.g., Nightingale NMR metabolomics) to mortality endpoints in cohort data, and per-cohort Cox results must be meta-analyzed into pooled HRs and 95% CIs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cox-proportional-hazards-mortality-modeling))
- **cox-proportional-hazards-regression**: Use when you have per-sample survival time/status merged with continuous molecular scores (e.g. GSVA) or clinical covariates and need to determine which factors independently predict survival. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cox-proportional-hazards-regression))
- **cox-proportional-hazards-survival-analysis**: Use when cohort-level survival summary statistics (e.g. 226 Nightingale NMR metabolic biomarkers vs. all-cause mortality) must be combined across cohorts via fixed-effect meta-analysis to select an independent, significant biomarker set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cox-proportional-hazards-survival-analysis))
- **cox-proportional-hazards-survival-analysis**: Use when input is a phyloseq object (biom.Rdata) or its four component files with survival time, censoring indicator, treatment, and covariate columns, and the goal is a covariate-adjusted Cox model of treatment or CLR-transformed microbial abundance on survival. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/cox-proportional-hazards-survival-analysis))
- **cox-proportional-hazards-survival-analysis**: Use when a bulk expression cohort (e.g. TCGA SKCM06 RNA-seq with clinical info) has matched event/time-to-event survival fields and you need per-feature hazard ratios with BH-adjusted significance for cell types or genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cox-proportional-hazards-survival-analysis))
- **cox-proportional-hazards-survival-modeling**: Use when pathway activity scores (e.g., PROGENy scores for EGFR, MAPK, PI3K, Hypoxia, Trail) must be tested against overall survival time and event status in a tumor cohort such as TCGA, with covariate adjustment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cox-proportional-hazards-survival-modeling--6df80f27b69e9b0f))
- **cox-proportional-hazards-survival-modeling**: Use when testing whether a candidate biomarker (e.g. binarized GEP weights or transferred embedding coordinates) has a statistically significant differential effect on patient outcomes between two treatment arms in a randomized trial with PFS/OS endpoints. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cox-proportional-hazards-survival-modeling--429b45914e3e864d))
- **cox-regression-model-fitting**: Use when you have expression or metabolomic feature matrices, paired with event indicators (e.g., disease recurrence, mortality) and follow-up times for a cohort of samples, and you need to derive risk scores or prognosis indices for survival prediction or patient stratification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cox-regression-model-fitting))
- **cox-survival-analysis**: Run Cox proportional hazards regression on clinical and molecular data. - **cp-accelerate**: Generate all remaining study deliverables in a single pass. Triggers: fast-forward, fill in the rest, generate remaining, accelerate, complete all deliverables. ([theMatthewNewman/cp-skills](https://github.com/theMatthewNewman/cp-skills/tree/HEAD/cp-accelerate))
- **cp-action**: Register a new drug discovery action in the Gibbs action registry. Guided scaffold: creates ActionDef, defines typed inputs/outputs, parameters, pre/postconditions, and regenerates the composability graph. Triggers: new action, register action, add action to registry, create ActionDef. ([theMatthewNewman/cp-skills](https://github.com/theMatthewNewman/cp-skills/tree/HEAD/cp-action))
- **cp-advance**: Create the next deliverable in the study workflow sequence. Triggers: continue, next step, advance, what is next, keep going. ([theMatthewNewman/cp-skills](https://github.com/theMatthewNewman/cp-skills/tree/HEAD/cp-advance))
- **cp-analysis-short**: Erstellt eine kompakte Multi-Source-SEO- und Performance-Analyse für eine Domain als selbstständige HTML-Datei. Verwende diesen Skill immer wenn der Nutzer sagt: «Kurzanalyse», «analysiere die Website», «wie läuft die Domain», «mach mir eine Analyse für X», «zeig mir den Status von X», «was läuft bei X» oder ähnliches — auch wenn keine explizite Quellenangabe gemacht wird. Der Skill zieht parallel Daten aus GSC, Sistrix, GA4 (adloop) und Screaming Frog und liefert einen strukturierten HTML-Report inkl. Kernerkenntnis und Top-5-Massnahmen. - **cp-analytics**: Разбирает статистику одной площадки: где взять данные (ВКонтакте — XLS/CSV и stats.get, Дзен — XLS, YouTube — Advanced mode с лимитом 500 строк, TikTok — XLSX/CSV, Instagram и Telegram — только руками), какие поля брать, как нормировать на охват, считать медиану, ловить выбросы двумя ступенями (2×–10× медианы разбирается как контент, свыше 10× сначала объясняется дистрибуцией) и раскладывать единицы по квадрантам «охват × вовлечённость». - **cp-analytics-cross**: Сводит несколько площадок в один отчёт и не даёт при этом соврать. Ядро — словарь несовместимости: «просмотр» означает разное на каждой площадке, «показ» — три разные сущности, ER считается от разных знаменателей, метрика для отчёта не равна метрике для денег. Абсолютные просмотры между площадками не сравнивает никогда, работает на нормированных величинах и динамике внутри площадки. Собирает отчёт клиенту: что выросло, что упало, что артефакт платформы, что требует решения, плюс обязательный раздел «чего мы не знаем». Опционально — простой HTML-дашборд без платных сервисов. - **cp-app**: Помогает выбрать между мобильным сайтом, PWA и нативным приложением, спланировать MVP, стек, выпуск, обновления и ASO. Требования магазинов проверяет на дату. Сайты ведёт cp-web, ботов — cp-bot. Use when the user asks whether to build a mobile app, how to plan an MVP, which stack to choose, or how to publish and promote an app. Триггеры — «мобильное приложение», «нужно ли приложение», «PWA или нативка», «выложить в магазин приложений», «RuStore», «ASO», «карточка приложения», «скриншоты для магазина», «обновление приложения», «сколько стоит приложение». - **cp-app**: Помогает решить, нужно ли мобильное приложение вообще, и если нужно — спланировать минимальную версию, описать её требованиями, выбрать стек под задачу и команду, спланировать выпуск, обновления и продвижение карточки. Начинает с честной развилки «мобильный сайт или PWA против нативного приложения»: приложение ради присутствия в магазине окупается редко.
